## Quick Start

```bash
# List all geography datasets
curl https://cdn.en.listbase.org/index.json | jq '.datasets[] | select(.category=="geography") | .id'

# Fetch a dataset
curl https://cdn.en.listbase.org/geography/geo-portugal-postal.json
```
